// @public
export type OgeEditorCommand = {
    readonly type: 'toggleMark';
    readonly mark: OgeEditorMarkName;
} | {
    readonly type: 'blockFormat';
    readonly format: OgeEditorBlockFormat;
    readonly level?: OgeEditorHeadingLevel;
    readonly toggle?: boolean;
} | {
    readonly type: 'list';
    readonly list: OgeEditorListKind;
} | {
    readonly type: 'indent';
} | {
    readonly type: 'outdent';
} | {
    readonly type: 'align';
    readonly align: OgeEditorAlign;
} | {
    readonly type: 'direction';
    readonly dir: OgeEditorDirection | null;
} | {
    readonly type: 'color';
    readonly color: string | null;
} | {
    readonly type: 'background';
    readonly color: string | null;
} | {
    readonly type: 'link';
    readonly href: string | null;
    readonly title?: string;
    readonly newTab?: boolean;
    readonly text?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'image';
    readonly src: string;
    readonly alt?: string;
    readonly width?: number;
    readonly height?: number;
} | {
    readonly type: 'horizontalRule';
} | {
    readonly type: 'clearFormatting';
} | {
    readonly type: 'undo';
} | {
    readonly type: 'redo';
} | {
    readonly type: 'selectAll';
} | {
    readonly type: 'insertText';
    readonly text: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'insertHtml';
    readonly html: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'splitBlock';
} | {
    readonly type: 'lineBreak';
};

// @public
export interface OgeEditorCommandExecutedEvent {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly command: OgeEditorCommand;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly event: Event | undefined;
}

export type OgeEditorCommandName = 'bold' | 'italic' | 'underline' | 'strike' | 'code' | 'subscript' | 'superscript' | 'paragraph' | 'heading1' | 'heading2' | 'heading3' | 'heading4' | 'heading5' | 'heading6' | 'blockquote' | 'codeBlock' | 'bulletList' | 'orderedList' | 'indent' | 'outdent' | 'alignStart' | 'alignCenter' | 'alignEnd' | 'alignJustify' | 'horizontalRule' | 'clearFormatting' | 'unlink' | 'undo' | 'redo' | 'selectAll';

}

// @public
export class OgeEditorCore {
    constructor(adapter: OgeReactivityAdapter, host: OgeEditorCoreHost);
    readonly active: () => OgeEditorActiveState;
    attach(root: HTMLElement): void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly canRedo: OgeReactiveCell<boolean>;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly canUndo: OgeReactiveCell<boolean>;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly characterCount: () => number;
    destroy(): void;
    detach(): void;
    readonly empty: () => boolean;
    exec(command: OgeEditorCommand, event?: Event, announce?: boolean): boolean;
    focus(): void;
    getHtml(): string;
    getText(): string;
    readonly html: () => string;
    insertHtml(html: string): void;
    insertText(text: string): void;
    readonly mac: boolean;
    moveToEnd(): void;
    redo(event?: Event, announce?: boolean): boolean;
    renderTo(root: HTMLElement): void;
    revive(root?: HTMLElement): void;
    setValue(html: string | null | undefined, options?: {
        readonly parser?: 'auto' | 'portable';
    }): void;
    readonly state: OgeReactiveCell<OgeEditorState>;
    syncSelectionFromDom(): void;
    undo(event?: Event, announce?: boolean): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly wordCount: () => number;
}

}

    kind: 'data';

} | {
    kind: 'group';

    text: string;

    kind: 'groupFooter';
    level: number;

} | {
    kind: 'total';

};

export interface OgeFileUploaderCoreOptions {
    readonly baseId: string;
    readonly config: () => OgeUploadConfig;
    readonly defaultAdapter: () => OgeUploadAdapter;
    readonly document?: () => Document | null;
    readonly emit: <K extends OgeFileUploaderEventName>(name: K, payload: OgeFileUploaderEvents[K]) => void;
    readonly messages: () => OgeUploadMessages;
    readonly nativeInput: () => HTMLInputElement | null;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly now?: () => number;
    readonly onChange: () => void;
    readonly onDirty: () => void;
    readonly onValueChange: (files: readonly File[]) => void;
    readonly props: () => OgeFileUploaderProps;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly timers?: OgeUploadTimers;
}

// @public

export class OgeGridEditingCore<T = unknown, TSlot = unknown, S = unknown> {
    constructor(deps: OgeGridEditingCoreDeps<T, TSlot, S>, rx: OgeReactivityAdapter);
    // (undocumented)
    addNewRow(): void;
    applyCellValues(writes: readonly OgeGridCellValueWrite[], options?: OgeGridApplyValuesOptions): Promise<number>;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly canAdd: () => boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    cancelActiveEditor(notify?: boolean): void;
    cancelEditing(): void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly canDelete: () => boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly canUpdate: () => boolean;
    commitActiveCell(): void;
    commitActiveRow(): void;
    commitAndNext(node: DataRowNode<T>, column: OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>, event: Event): void;
    // Warning: (ae-forgotten-export) The symbol "DataRowNode" needs to be exported by the entry point index.d.ts
    protected dataNodeOf(key: RowKey): DataRowNode<T> | undefined;
    // (undocumented)
    deleteRow(node: DataRowNode<T>, event?: Event): void;
    // (undocumented)
    protected readonly deps: OgeGridEditingCoreDeps<T, TSlot, S>;
    // (undocumented)
    discardAllChanges(notify?: boolean): void;
    displayValue(node: DataRowNode<T>, column: OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>): unknown;
    protected draftRowOf(node: DataRowNode<T>): T;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly editingOptions: () => OgeEditingOptions | null;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly editMode: () => OgeEditingOptions['mode'] | null;
    protected editorValue(raw: unknown, column: OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>, original?: unknown): unknown;
    readonly history: OgeGridEditHistory;
    // (undocumented)
    isCellDirty(node: DataRowNode<T>, column: OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    isCellEditorOpen(node: DataRowNode<T>, column: OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>): boolean;
    isFormRow(key: RowKey): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    isRowEditing(key: RowKey): boolean;
    lookupItemsFor(node: DataRowNode<T>, column: OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>): readonly LookupItem[] | undefined;
    notifyEditingStart(key: RowKey, row: T | undefined, field?: string): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    onEditorBlur(): void;
    redo(): Promise<number>;
    // (undocumented)
    protected runSave(changes: OgeDataChange<T>[]): Promise<void>;
    saveAllChanges(): void;
    // (undocumented)
    startRowEdit(node: DataRowNode<T>, event?: Event): void;
    undo(): Promise<number>;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export interface OgeGridEditingCoreDeps<T, TSlot = unknown, S = unknown> {
    columns: () => readonly OgeGridResolvedColumn<T, TSlot, S>[];
    confirmDeleteMessage: () => string;
    editing: () => false | OgeEditingOptions;
    editors: OgeGridEditorBridge;
    // (undocumented)
    events: {
        savingChanges(event: OgeSavingChangesEvent<T>): void;
        savedChanges?(event: OgeSavedChangesEvent<T>): void;
        editingStart?(event: OgeEditingStartEvent<T>): void;
        rowInserting?(event: OgeRowInsertingEvent): void;
        rowInserted?(event: OgeRowInsertedEvent): void;
        rowUpdating?(event: OgeRowUpdatingEvent<T>): void;
        rowUpdated?(event: OgeRowUpdatedEvent): void;
        rowRemoving?(event: OgeRowRemovingEvent<T>): void;
        rowRemoved?(event: OgeRowRemovedEvent): void;
        editCanceled?(): void;
        dataError?(error: unknown): void;
        validationFailed?(invalid: readonly OgeGridInvalidEditor[]): void;
    };
    flatNodes: () => readonly RowNode<T>[];
    reload(): void;
    source: () => DataSource<T> | null;
    state: OgeGridEditingState;
}

// @public
export class OgeGridEditingState {
    constructor(rx: OgeReactivityAdapter);
    readonly added: () => readonly RowKey[];
    // (undocumented)
    addRow(key: RowKey): void;
    // (undocumented)
    changeFor(key: RowKey, field: string): unknown;
    readonly changes: () => ReadonlyMap<RowKey, Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>>;
    clearChange(key: RowKey, field: string): void;
    // (undocumented)
    clearPending(): void;
    // (undocumented)
    discardRow(key: RowKey): void;
    // (undocumented)
    dropAdded(key: RowKey): void;
    readonly editCell: () => OgeEditingCell | null;
    readonly editRowKey: () => RowKey | null;
    // (undocumented)
    hasChange(key: RowKey, field: string): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly hasPending: () => boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    isAdded(key: RowKey): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    isCellEditing(key: RowKey, field: string): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    isRemoved(key: RowKey): boolean;
    readonly removed: () => ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
    // (undocumented)
    setChange(key: RowKey, field: string, value: unknown): void;
    // (undocumented)
    setRowChanges(key: RowKey, values: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    // (undocumented)
    startCell(key: RowKey, field: string): void;
    // (undocumented)
    startRow(key: RowKey): void;
    // (undocumented)
    stopEditor(): void;
    // (undocumented)
    toggleRemoved(key: RowKey): void;
}

// @public
export interface OgeGridEditorBridge {
    editorState(key: RowKey, field: string): OgeGridEditorState | undefined;
    markTouched(key: RowKey, field: string): void;
    rowValues(key: RowKey): ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>;
    validateValue?(key: RowKey, field: string, value: unknown, row: unknown): boolean | Promise<boolean>;
    whenValidated?(key: RowKey, field: string): Promise<void>;
}

// @public
export interface OgeGridEditorState {
    // (undocumented)
    invalid: boolean;
    pending?: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    value: unknown;
}

// @public
export interface OgeGridEditRecord {
    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)
    readonly before: unknown;
    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)

}

    readonly direction: 1 | -1;
};

// @public

    constructor(rx: OgeReactivityAdapter);

    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)

}

}

// @public
export class OgeGridStateCore {
    constructor(rx: OgeReactivityAdapter);
    applySnapshot(snapshot: GridStateSnapshot): void;
    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)
    readonly editing: OgeGridEditingState;
    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)
    readonly paging: OgeGridPagingState;
    reconcile(): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly selection: OgeGridSelectionState;
    // Warning: (ae-forgotten-export) The symbol "GridStateSnapshot" needs to be exported by the entry point index.d.ts
    readonly snapshot: () => GridStateSnapshot;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly sort: OgeGridSortState;
}

// @public
export class OgeGridStatePersistenceCore<S> {
    constructor(options: OgeGridStatePersistenceOptions<S>);
    dispose(): void;

    restore(key: string | undefined): void;
}

    // (undocumented)
    apply(snapshot: S): void;
    debounceMs?: number;
    onChange?: (snapshot: S) => void;

    sanitize?: (value: unknown) => S | null;
    snapshot: () => S;
    stateKey: () => string | undefined;
    // (undocumented)

}

// @public
export class OgeListBoxCore<TItem> extends OgeSelectListCore<TItem> {
    constructor(boxDeps: OgeListBoxCoreDeps<TItem>, rx: OgeReactivityAdapter);
    activateItem(item: TItem): boolean;
    allVisibleSelected(): boolean;
    readonly anchorIndex: OgeReactiveCell<number>;
    clickOption(index: number, input?: OgeListBoxClickInput): unknown;
    ensureActive(): void;
    handleKey(input: OgeListBoxKeyInput, editable?: boolean): OgeListBoxKeyResult;
    isSelected(item: TItem): boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    get multiple(): boolean;
    reorderAt(item: TItem, target: TItem, position: 'before' | 'after'): OgeListBoxReorder | null;
    reorderKey(input: OgeListBoxKeyInput): {
        handled: boolean;
        reorder?: OgeListBoxReorder;
    };
    resetTypeAhead(): void;
    selectAllValue(): unknown;
    selectedItems(): TItem[];
    selectedValues(): readonly unknown[];
    selectionDelta(previous: unknown, next: unknown): {
        changed: boolean;
        added: TItem[];
        removed: TItem[];
    };
    toValue(values: readonly unknown[]): unknown;
    unselectAllValue(): unknown;
}

}

// @public
export interface OgeToastAction<D = unknown> {
    handler?: (event: OgeToastActionEvent<D>) => void;
    text: string;
}

// @public
export interface OgeToastActionEvent<D = unknown> {
    readonly data?: D;
    readonly event: MouseEvent;
}

// @public
export type OgeToastAnnounce = 'polite' | 'assertive' | 'off';

// @public
export interface OgeToastBaseOptions<D = unknown> {
    action?: OgeToastAction<D>;
    announce?: OgeToastAnnounce;
    announceText?: string;
    closable?: boolean;
    closeOnClick?: boolean;
    coalesce?: boolean;
    cssClass?: string;
    data?: D;
    displayTime?: number;
    id?: string;
    loading?: boolean;
    message: string;
    position?: OgeToastPosition;
    progressBar?: boolean;
    severity?: OgeToastSeverity;
    sticky?: boolean;
    title?: string;
}

// @public
export type OgeToastBasePatch<D = unknown> = Partial<Omit<OgeToastBaseOptions<D>, 'position' | 'id'>> & {
    [custom: string]: unknown;
};

// @public
export interface OgeToastClosedEvent {
    readonly reason: OgeToastCloseReason;
}

// @public
export type OgeToastCloseReason = 'timeout' | 'closeButton' | 'click' | 'action' | 'api' | 'clear';

// @public
export class OgeToastCore<O extends AnyToastOptions = OgeToastBaseOptions> {
    constructor(options: OgeToastCoreOptions);
    clear(position?: OgeToastPosition): void;
    destroy(): void;
    dismiss(entry: OgeToastEntry<O>, reason: OgeToastCloseReason): void;
    entries: OgeToastEntry<O>[];
    // (undocumented)
    error(message: string, options?: Omit<O, 'message' | 'severity'>): OgeToastRef;
    // (undocumented)
    info(message: string, options?: Omit<O, 'message' | 'severity'>): OgeToastRef;
    markReady(entries: readonly OgeToastEntry<O>[]): void;
    pause(entry: OgeToastEntry<O>): void;
    promise<T>(promise: Promise<T>, options: OgeToastPromiseBaseOptions<T, O>): OgeToastRef;
    regions(): OgeToastRegion<O>[];
    resume(entry: OgeToastEntry<O>): void;
    show(toast: string | O): OgeToastRef;
    success(message: string, options?: Omit<O, 'message' | 'severity'>): OgeToastRef;
    // (undocumented)
    warning(message: string, options?: Omit<O, 'message' | 'severity'>): OgeToastRef;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export interface OgeToastCoreOptions {
    announce: (mode: 'polite' | 'assertive', text: string) => void;
    defaults: () => OgeToastDefaults;
    onChange: () => void;
}

// @public
export interface OgeToastDefaults {
    coalesceDuplicates: boolean;
    displayTime: number;
    maxVisible: number;
    position: OgeToastPosition;
    progressBar: boolean;
}

// @public
export interface OgeToastEntry<O extends AnyToastOptions = OgeToastBaseOptions> {
    closing: boolean;
    count: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly key: number;
    // (undocumented)
    options: OgeResolvedToastOptions<O>;
    // @internal (undocumented)
    pauseCauses: number;
    progressStyle: OgeToastProgressStyle | null;
    ready: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly ref: OgeToastRef;
    // @internal (undocumented)
    remaining: number;
    // @internal (undocumented)
    resolveClosed: (event: OgeToastClosedEvent) => void;
    // @internal (undocumented)
    startedAt: number;
    // @internal (undocumented)
    timerId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;
    // @internal (undocumented)
    total: number;
}

// @public
export type OgeToastPosition = 'top-start' | 'top-center' | 'top-end' | 'bottom-start' | 'bottom-center' | 'bottom-end';

// @public
export interface OgeToastProgressStyle {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly transform: string;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly transition: string;
}

// @public
export type OgeToastPromiseBaseOptions<T, O extends AnyToastOptions> = Omit<O, 'message' | 'severity' | 'loading'> & {
    loading: string;
    success: string | ((value: T) => string | OgeToastBasePatch);
    error: string | ((error: unknown) => string | OgeToastBasePatch);
};

// @public
export class OgeToastRef<D = unknown> {
    constructor(closed: Promise<OgeToastClosedEvent>);
    close(): void;
    // @internal
    _close: () => void;
    readonly closed: Promise<OgeToastClosedEvent>;
    update(patch: OgeToastBasePatch<D>): void;
    // @internal
    _update: (patch: OgeToastBasePatch<D>) => void;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export interface OgeToastRegion<O extends AnyToastOptions> {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly position: OgeToastPosition;
    readonly visible: readonly OgeToastEntry<O>[];
}

// @public
export type OgeToastSeverity = 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';

// @public
export type OgeTreeKeyAction<T> =
/** Move the roving tab stop (and the scroll window) to this row. */
    {
    readonly kind: 'focus';
    readonly index: number;
}
/** Flip this row's selected state. */
| {
    readonly kind: 'toggle-selection';
    readonly index: number;
}
/** Add every enabled row in the inclusive range to the selection. */
| {
    readonly kind: 'select-range';
    readonly from: number;
    readonly to: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: 'select-all';
} | {
    readonly kind: 'expand';
    readonly key: RowKey;
} | {
    readonly kind: 'collapse';
    readonly key: RowKey;
} | {
    readonly kind: 'toggle-expansion';
    readonly key: RowKey;
}
/** Report the row as clicked — Enter is a click in the APG map. */
| {
    readonly kind: 'item-click';
    readonly node: OgeTreeViewNode<T>;
}
/** Replace the expansion set outright (the `*` shortcut). */
| {
    readonly kind: 'set-expanded';
    readonly expanded: ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
}
/** Reveal the next page under a "Load more" row (`childPageSize`). */
| {
    readonly kind: 'load-more';
    readonly node: OgeTreeViewNode<T>;
};

// @public
export interface OgeTreeKeyInput<T> {
    // (undocumented)
    allowExpandAll?: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    altKey?: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    ctrlKey?: boolean;
    current: number;
    // (undocumented)
    expandableKeys: ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
    // (undocumented)
    expanded: ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
    // (undocumented)
    index: TreeIndex<T>;
    // (undocumented)
    key: string;
    // (undocumented)
    keyOf: (row: T) => RowKey;
    // (undocumented)
    metaKey?: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    nodes: readonly OgeTreeViewNode<T>[];
    pushTypeAhead: (char: string) => string;
    // (undocumented)
    selectionMode: OgeTreeSelectionMode;
    // (undocumented)
    shiftKey?: boolean;
}

    // (undocumented)

    // (undocumented)

}

export class OgeTreeListCore<T> {
    constructor(deps: OgeTreeListCoreDeps<T>, rx: OgeReactivityAdapter);
    // (undocumented)
    readonly allSelected: () => boolean;
    applyDrop(draggedKey: RowKey, targetKey: RowKey, position: OgeTreeDropPosition, reload: () => void): OgeTreeRowReparentEvent<T> | null;
    applyExpandedRowKeys(keys: readonly RowKey[]): void;
    applyExpansionSnapshot(snapshot: TreeListStateSnapshot): void;
    readonly checkStates: () => ReadonlyMap<RowKey, CheckState>;
    readonly childLoadBase: () => LoadOptions;
    clipboardText(columns: readonly OgeExportColumn<T>[]): string;
    // (undocumented)
    collapseAll(): void;
    // (undocumented)
    collapseRow(key: RowKey): void;
    connect(data: readonly T[] | DataSource<T>, sortValues?: Readonly<Record<string, (row: T) => unknown>>): DataSource<T>;
    connectInputs(): void;
    readonly dataKeys: () => readonly RowKey[];
    dataNodeByKey(key: RowKey): DataRowNode<T> | undefined;
    readonly deferredLoader: OgeGridDeferredChildrenCore<T>;
    distinctValues(accessor: (row: T) => unknown, limit: number, field?: string): readonly unknown[];
    // (undocumented)
    readonly effLoadMode: () => OgeTreeLoadMode;
    readonly effPageSize: () => number | null;
    readonly expandableKeys: () => ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
    // (undocumented)
    expandAll(): void;
    readonly expandedSet: () => ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
    expandRow(key: RowKey): void;
    // Warning: (ae-forgotten-export) The symbol "TreeListStateSnapshot" needs to be exported by the entry point index.d.ts
    expansionSnapshot(): NonNullable<TreeListStateSnapshot['expansion']>;
    readonly filterExpandedKeys: () => ReadonlySet<RowKey> | null;
    readonly filterPredicate: () => ((row: T) => boolean) | null;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly firstDataRow: () => T | undefined;
    readonly flatNodes: () => RowNode<T>[];
    forEachNode(callback: (row: T, key: RowKey, parentKey: RowKey | null) => void): void;
    getExportData(columns: readonly OgeTreeExportSourceColumn<T>[], messages: OgeTreeBooleanMessages, options?: OgeTreeExportBuildOptions): OgeTreeExportData<T>;
    getNodeByKey(key: RowKey): T | undefined;
    getSelectedRowKeys(mode?: OgeTreeSelectedKeysMode): RowKey[];
    getSelectedRowsData(mode?: OgeTreeSelectedKeysMode): T[];
    getVisibleRows(): readonly T[];
    // (undocumented)
    readonly hasChildrenHint: () => ((row: T) => boolean | undefined) | undefined;
    hasUnloadedDescendants(key: RowKey): boolean;
    readonly indexRows: () => readonly T[];
    readonly innerSource: () => DataSource<T> | null;
    // (undocumented)
    isRowExpanded(key: RowKey): boolean;
    isValidDropTarget(draggedKey: RowKey | null, targetKey: RowKey): boolean;
    keyboardMoveTarget(key: RowKey, move: OgeTreeRowKeyMove): {
        targetKey: RowKey;
        position: OgeTreeDropPosition;
    } | null;
    keyboardTreeHooks(toggle: (node: DataRowNode<T>, expand: boolean) => void): OgeGridKeyboardNavTreeHooks;
    readonly keyToFlatIndex: () => ReadonlyMap<RowKey, number>;
    readonly lazyKeyField: () => string | null;
    readonly lazyParentField: () => string | null;
    loadSubtree(rootKey: RowKey): Promise<void>;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly nestedItemsOf: () => ((row: T) => readonly T[] | undefined) | null;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly pageCount: () => number;
    pageSize(): number;
    readonly parentIdOf: () => (row: T) => unknown;
    readonly pendingChildRequests: () => readonly OgePendingChildRequest[];
    readonly recursiveSummaries: () => ReadonlyMap<RowKey, readonly SummaryValue[]>;
    refresh(): void;
    remoteDistinctValues(field: string): readonly unknown[] | undefined;
    readonly remoteFilteringActive: () => boolean;
    remoteFilterInputs(): void;
    readonly remoteFilterRows: () => readonly T[];
    readonly renderedRowCount: () => number;
    readonly renderNodes: () => readonly RowNode<T>[];
    requestDistinctValues(field: string): void;
    requestToggle(node: DataRowNode<T>, expand: boolean, notify: OgeTreeToggleNotifier<T>): boolean;
    revealRow(key: RowKey): number | undefined;
    rowCheckState(key: RowKey): CheckState;
    readonly rowKeyOf: () => (row: T) => RowKey;
    rowPlacement(key: RowKey): {
        level: number;
        position: number;
        total: number;
    } | null;
    selectAll(): void;
    setPageIndex(index: number): void;
    setPageSize(size: number): void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly someSelected: () => boolean;
    stageNewRowParent(key: RowKey, parentKey: RowKey | undefined): void;
    readonly summaryRows: () => readonly T[];
    syncRemoteFilter(): void;
    readonly toggledKeys: () => ReadonlySet<RowKey>;
    toggleSelection(key: RowKey): void;
    readonly totalCount: () => number;
    readonly totalSummaries: () => readonly SummaryValue[];
    readonly treeIndex: () => TreeIndex<T>;
    readonly visibleKeys: () => ReadonlySet<RowKey> | null;
}

// @public
export type OgeUploadQueueEvent = {
    readonly type: 'started';
    readonly uids: readonly string[];
} | {
    readonly type: 'progress';
    readonly uid: string;
    readonly loaded: number;
    readonly total: number;
} | {
    readonly type: 'chunkStarted';
    readonly uid: string;
    readonly index: number;
    readonly total: number;
} | {
    readonly type: 'chunkDone';
    readonly uid: string;
    readonly index: number;
    readonly total: number;
    readonly response: unknown;
} | {
    readonly type: 'chunkFailed';
    readonly uid: string;
    readonly index: number;
    readonly message: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'done';
    readonly uid: string;
    readonly response: unknown;
    readonly httpStatus: number;
} | {
    readonly type: 'failed';
    readonly uid: string;
    readonly message: string;
    readonly httpStatus: number | null;
    readonly response: unknown;
} | {
    readonly type: 'aborted';
    readonly uid: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'paused';
    readonly uid: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'resumed';
    readonly uid: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'idle';
};

// @public (undocumented)
export interface OgeUploadQueueOptions {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly adapter: OgeUploadAdapter;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly autoRetry: OgeResolvedRetryOptions | null;
    readonly batch: boolean;
    readonly buildRequest: (tasks: readonly OgeUploadTask[], chunk: OgeUploadChunk | null, chunkTotal: number) => OgeUploadRequest | null;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly chunk: OgeResolvedChunkOptions | null;
    readonly concurrency: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly onEvent: (event: OgeUploadQueueEvent) => void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly timers: OgeUploadTimers;
}

}

// @public
export interface OgeValidationContext {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly data: Record<string, unknown>;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly value: unknown;
}

// @public
export type OgeValidationRule = {
    readonly type: 'required';
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'email';
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'numeric';
    readonly min?: number;
    readonly max?: number;
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'stringLength';
    readonly min?: number;
    readonly max?: number;
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'pattern';
    readonly pattern: RegExp;
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'range';
    readonly min?: Date;
    readonly max?: Date;
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'compare';
    readonly comparisonTarget: string | ((data: Record<string, unknown>) => unknown);
    readonly comparisonType?: OgeComparisonType;
    readonly ignoreEmptyValue?: boolean;
    readonly message?: string;
} | {
    readonly type: 'custom';
    readonly validate: (context: OgeValidationContext) => string | null;
} | {
    readonly type: 'async';
    readonly validate: (value: unknown) => Promise<string | null>;
    readonly message?: string;
};

}

export type OgeWindowChangeSource = 'pointer' | 'keyboard' | 'api';

export interface OgeWindowClosedEvent {
    readonly reason: OgeWindowCloseReason;
}

// @public
export type OgeWindowCloseReason = 'api' | 'escape' | 'closeButton';

// @public
export interface OgeWindowClosingEvent {
    cancel: boolean;
    readonly reason: OgeWindowCloseReason;
}

// @public
export class OgeWindowCore {
    constructor(options: OgeWindowCoreOptions);
    readonly active: OgeReactiveCell<boolean>;
    attach(): void;
    bringToFront(): void;
    center(): void;
    detach(): void;
    readonly interaction: OgeReactiveCell<'move' | 'resize' | null>;
    get isAttached(): boolean;
    keydown(event: OgeWindowKeyInput): boolean;
    readonly layer: OgeReactiveCell<number>;
    maximize(): boolean;
    minimize(): boolean;
    moveTo(x: number, y: number, source?: OgeWindowChangeSource, event?: Event): void;
    place(force?: OgeWindowPlacement): void;
    readonly rect: OgeReactiveCell<OgeWindowRect | null>;
    resizeTo(width: number, height: number, source?: OgeWindowChangeSource, event?: Event): void;
    restore(): boolean;
    setState(next: OgeWindowState): boolean;
    startDrag(event: OgeWindowPointerInput): void;
    startResize(event: OgeWindowPointerInput, edge: OgeWindowResizeEdge): void;
    readonly state: OgeReactiveCell<OgeWindowState>;
    syncPosition(): void;
    toggleMaximize(): boolean;
}

// @public
export interface OgeWindowCoreOptions {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly adapter: OgeReactivityAdapter;
    readonly announce?: (message: string) => void;
    readonly initialState?: OgeWindowState;
    readonly onActivated?: () => void;
    readonly onEscape?: (event: OgeWindowKeyInput) => void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly onMoved?: (event: OgeWindowMovedEvent) => void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly onResized?: (event: OgeWindowResizedEvent) => void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly onStateChanged?: (event: OgeWindowStateChangedEvent) => void;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly onStateChanging?: (event: OgeWindowStateChangingEvent) => void;
    readonly panel: () => HTMLElement | null;
    readonly props: () => OgeWindowCoreProps;
}

// @public
export interface OgeWindowCoreProps {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly closeOnEscape: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly draggable: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly keepInViewport: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly maxHeight?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly maxWidth?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly messages?: Partial<OgeOverlayMessages>;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly minHeight?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly minWidth?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly placement: OgeWindowPlacement;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly position?: OgeWindowPosition | null;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly resizable: boolean;
}

// @public
export type OgeWindowKeyCommand = {
    readonly type: 'move';
    readonly dx: number;
    readonly dy: number;
} | {
    readonly type: 'resize';
    readonly dw: number;
    readonly dh: number;
} | {
    readonly type: 'state';
    readonly direction: 'up' | 'down';
};
